# Jacked — Every Template, Day by Day

All **125 templates**, every training day, every lift, with sets and reps.
3,521 exercise slots · 9,418 working sets.

Each template is one week. The week repeats for the whole block — load progresses, the layout doesn't. Loads are blank on purpose: the template is a muscle-order skeleton, what you lift is yours.

## Contents

- Women's — Full body (10)
- Women's — Lower body emphasis (5)
- Women's — Upper body emphasis (5)
- Women's — Lower body specialty (20)
- Women's — Upper body specialty (26)
- Women's — Dumbbells only, at home (5)
- Men's — Full body (5)
- Men's — Lower body emphasis (5)
- Men's — Upper body emphasis (5)
- Men's — Lower body specialty (9)
- Men's — Upper body specialty (25)
- Men's — Dumbbells only, at home (5)
